Evaluated on trajectory-tracking tasks in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, CALOS reduces lateral tracking error by 55-60% relative to an unconstrained Proximal Policy Optimization baseline while achieving zero attitude-constraint violations on the training trajectory. By restricting exploration to safe regions of the state space, the safety layer also accelerates training convergence and improves data efficiency without producing suboptimal policies.

</details>


### [107] [DRT&R: Direct Radar Teach & Repeat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17766)
*Alexander Krawciw,Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DRT&R，一种基于旋转雷达的直接导航栈，通过结合直接雷达处理与局部建图以最大化信息保留，在道路与非道路环境中实现SOTA定位与跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达导航对雨雪、尘雾、烟等恶劣条件具有较强的鲁棒性，而激光雷达在这些条件下容易失效。现有直接方法虽在道路全局定位上表现提升，但尚未应用于非道路环境或闭环系统；且全局地图会平滑掉依赖视角的雷达特征，损失可用于定位的信息。

Method: 提出Direct Radar Teach & Repeat（DRT&R），将直接雷达处理与局部建图相结合，构建直接旋转雷达导航栈，以最大化保留依赖视角的雷达信息。

Result: 在344 km道路数据和20 km非道路数据上，多数场景定位误差约4 cm，几何退化与稀疏环境下约12 cm；在Clearpath Warthog非道路车辆上结合MPC控制器进行超过10 km的自主闭环测试，实时运行并达到SOTA跟踪性能。

Conclusion: DRT&R在道路和非道路环境中均实现SOTA定位性能，并验证了其在真实闭环自主导航中的实时性与跟踪能力。

Abstract: Radar-based navigation is appealing for its robustness to adverse conditions involving airborne particles, such as precipitation, dust, fog, and smoke, that can cause lidar-based systems to fail. Recently, direct methods that retain and use the entire radar scan rather than sparse points have improved on-road global localization performance. However, they have yet to be deployed in off-road environments or in closed-loop systems. Additionally, even direct global maps may lose information: their global nature leads to a smoothing out of viewpoint-dependent radar artifacts, which can provide pose information when mapping and localization occur along similar trajectories. This paper introduces Direct Radar Teach & Repeat (DRT&R): a direct spinning radar-based navigation stack that maximizes the amount of retained information by combining direct radar processing with local mapping. DRT&R yields state-of-the-art (SOTA) localization performance in both on-road and off-road environments. Using 344 km of on-road data and 20 km of off-road data, DRT&R is able to localize to within 4 cm in most on-road and off-road conditions, and 12 cm in geometrically degenerate and sparse environments. DRT&R is also evaluated autonomously in closed loop with an MPC controller for more than 10 km using a Clearpath Warthog off-road vehicle, demonstrating that it runs in real time and achieves SOTA tracking performance for off-road radar navigation.

</details>


### [108] [HINT-Plan: Human Intention-Aware Robot Task Planning in Context-Rich Environments using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17771)
*Yuchen Liu,Luigi Palmieri,Lujun Li,Radu State,Ilche Georgievski,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HINT-Plan，将人类意图预测融入机器人任务规划，利用VLM从图像中推断高层人类意图并转为目标状态，结合分层场景图与形式化规划语言求解人机联合任务规划问题，在仿真中成功率69.71%，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将人类意识融入移动机器人决策的方法主要聚焦于低层运动规划中的碰撞避免，忽视了人类存在所带来的人类高层行为与意图方面的挑战。为填补这一空白，作者希望把人类意图预测整合进机器人任务规划层面。

Method: 提出HINT-Plan方法：使用视觉语言模型（VLM）从第三人称图像观测中预测高层人类意图，将其转换为目标状态，并求解联合任务规划问题；同时采用分层场景图（SG）作为环境的高层表示以实现上下文丰富环境中的场景感知，并将环境拓扑与可操作知识翻译为形式化规划语言，保证规划可执行。

Result: 在照片级真实感仿真环境中评估，HINT-Plan在人机联合任务规划中取得69.71%的总体成功率，较基线最高提升35.29%，同时减少了功能性冲突。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将推断出的人类意图显式地纳入形式化多智能体任务规划，对于实现主动的人类感知机器人决策是有效的。

Abstract: Approaches to incorporating human awareness into mobile robot decision-making mainly focus on collision avoidance in low-level motion planning, often overlooking the challenges posed by human presence and high-level behavior. To address this vacancy, we present HINT-Plan, a novel approach to integrate human intention prediction into robot task planning. HINT-Plan employs Vision Language Models (VLMs) to anticipate high-level human intentions from third-person image observations, convert them into goal states, and solve joint task-planning problems. To effectively enable scene awareness in context-rich environments, we use hierarchical Scene Graphs (SGs) as high-level representations of the environment, and translate environmental topology and actionable knowledge into formal planning language to ensure executable plans. Evaluated in a photorealistic simulation, HINT-Plan achieves an overall success rate of 69.71% in joint human-robot task planning, substantially outperforming the baselines by up to 35.29%, while also reducing functional conflicts. The results show the effectiveness of explicitly incorporating inferred human intentions into formal multi-agent task planning for proactive human-aware robot decision-making.

</details>


### [109] [SPROUT: The Open-Source Soft Growing Robot for Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17781)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Ciera McFarland,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Chad Council,Megan Richardson,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SPROUT（Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit），一款面向搜索救援的软体机器人系统，经过与专业城市搜救团队多年合作迭代，具备在粉尘、潮湿和孤立环境中作业的能力，并公开代码、零件清单与装配说明以促进社区复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人长期被认为是搜索救援的理想方案，但从实验室走向现场存在显著障碍，缺乏可复制、可靠的软体机器人系统。为填补这一空白，作者提出SPROUT系统，旨在提供可复现、模块化且适应恶劣环境的软体机器人平台。

Method: 论文给出SPROUT的完整设计，包含模块化硬件、基于ROS 2的软件栈、任务专用载荷与控制功能，并提供代码、零件清单、CAD装配与搭建说明等补充材料。系统经过与城市搜救专业人员的多年互动迭代，设计目标为在粉尘、潮湿、孤立条件下运行。作者在急救人员使用的倒塌建筑训练场地中开展多种具有挑战性的检测与通过性任务演示。

Result: SPROUT成功完成了一系列具有挑战性的检测与通过任务，演示场景为急救人员使用的倒塌结构训练场地。系统可支持任务专用载荷与控制功能，并可扩展到基础设施检测、考古等搜索救援之外的领域。

Conclusion: SPROUT通过模块化软硬件设计与开放补充材料，降低了软体机器人从实验室到现场的复制门槛，展示了在真实救援训练环境中的适用性，并为更广泛的机器人研究社区构建自有系统以及跨领域应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Soft robotic systems have long been theorized as ideal candidates for use in search and rescue operations; however, there have been significant barriers to entry in graduating soft robotic systems from the laboratory to the field. To address this gap in replicable, reliable soft robot systems, we present the designs for SPROUT, the Soft Pathfinding Robotic Observation Unit. The system has matured over years of interaction with professional urban search and rescue communities, with the goal of operating in dusty, wet, and isolated conditions. This manuscript contains supplementary material, including code, parts manifests, CAD assemblies, and build instructions to allow the broader robotics research community to build their own SPROUT systems. The modular hardware and ROS 2-based software stack support task-specific payloads and control functions, allowing SPROUT to be adapted for applications beyond search and rescue, including infrastructure inspection and archaeology. Here, we demonstrate SPROUT performing a variety of challenging inspection and traversal tasks in collapsed structure training sites used by first responders. The main project page is available online at https://sprout-mitll.github.io/sprout/.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Multi-Humanoid Pickup and Transport via Decentralized Object-Centric Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17824)
*Bikram Pandit,Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以物体为中心的分布式控制方法，通过局部附着区域与无夹爪双臂捏取实现多尺寸、多几何物体的人形机器人协作搬运，并验证了从单机到多机的迁移与仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个人形机器人协作搬运不同尺寸、重量和几何形状的物体时，所需机器人队伍规模各异，传统方法往往需要针对每个任务重新设计控制策略，缺乏通用性与可扩展性。

Method: 采用去中心化的物体中心控制：为每个人形机器人分配共享物体上的局部附着区域，通过无夹爪双臂捏取学习完成拾取与运输。该基于附着的接口提供了统一控制抽象，涵盖单机拾取、多机协作运输与机器人间交接，无需按任务重新设计。

Result: 仅用单机拾取训练的策略已能非平凡地迁移到协作场景；显式多机训练进一步提升性能，表明共享物体耦合带来的协调动力学值得直接学习。在仿真中跨不同队伍规模与物体几何验证，并在硬件上实现仿真到现实迁移，使真实人形机器人完成协作操作任务。

Conclusion: 基于附着区域的物体中心控制抽象能够以统一方式支撑单机与多机协作操作，且单机训练具备良好迁移性，多机训练可进一步增益，方法具备仿真到现实的可落地性。

Abstract: We study cooperative multi-humanoid pickup and transport of objects with varying size, weight, and geometry, requiring robot teams of different sizes. Our approach uses decentralized object-centric control, where each humanoid is assigned a local attachment region on the shared object and learns to realize pickup and transport through gripperless bimanual pinching. This attachment-based interface provides a common control abstraction spanning single-robot pickup, cooperative multi-robot transport, and robot-to-robot handover, without per-task redesign. We find that policies trained only on single-robot pickup already transfer nontrivially to cooperative settings, suggesting that this abstraction captures much of the structure needed for coordination. At the same time, explicit multi-robot training further improves performance, showing that shared-object coupling introduces coordination dynamics that are beneficial to learn directly. We validate the approach in simulation across varying team sizes and object geometries, and demonstrate sim-to-real transfer on hardware, where the learned controllers enable real humanoids to perform cooperative manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Adaptive-MHE : A Sampling-Based Adaptive MPC for Legged Loco-Manipulation via Moving Horizon Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17832)
*Hossein Keshavarz,Alejandro Ramirez-Serrano,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive-MHE，一种基于移动地平线估计（MHE）的在线采样式系统辨识框架，可在腿部机器人上估计物体与地形的物理参数并与采样式MPC耦合，实现自适应loco-manipulation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿部机器人虽能穿越多种地形，但生成有效的loco-manipulation行为仍具挑战。核心难点是物体与地形参数对机器人未知，仿真与现实的参数不匹配造成sim-to-real gap，降低控制性能。传统Sys-ID依赖可微动力学假设，不适用于接触丰富的腿部系统；采样式Sys-ID虽回避该假设，但多为离线应用，无法随环境变化自适应。

Method: 提出Adaptive-MHE：基于移动地平线估计（MHE）的在线采样式Sys-ID框架。通过大规模并行rollout直接匹配仿真与记录的轨迹状态，在线估计环境中物体与地形的物理参数（如质量、摩擦），并将该估计与采样式模型预测控制器耦合。

Result: 在仿真与硬件实验中，该框架持续优于各基线方法，并达到与可访问真值参数的控制器相当的性能。

Conclusion: 在线采样式Sys-ID与采样式MPC的结合可有效应对变化且不确定的环境，实现自适应loco-manipulation，缩小sim-to-real差距。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated a remarkable ability to traverse various terrains, yet generating effective loco-manipulation behaviors remains challenging. A key difficulty is that object and terrain parameters are typically unknown to the robot, and mismatches between these parameters and their simulated counterparts introduce a sim-to-real gap that degrades control performance. Classical system identification (Sys-ID) methods often assume differentiable dynamics, an assumption that does not hold for contact-rich legged systems. Sampling-based Sys-ID avoids this restriction by directly matching simulated and recorded state trajectories through massively parallel rollouts, but existing approaches are typically applied offline and do not adapt as environmental conditions change. We present Adaptive-MHE an online sampling-based Sys-ID framework, based on moving horizon estimation (MHE), that estimates the physical parameters of objects and terrain in the environment (e.g., mass, friction) and couples this estimate with a sampling-based model predictive controller, enabling adaptive loco-manipulation in changing and uncertain environments. In simulation and hardware experiments, our framework consistently outperforms baselines and matches the performance of a controller with access to ground-truth parameters.

</details>


### [112] [VCTP: Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17834)
*Akshay Naik,Ramavarapu S. Sreenivas,Dustin Nottage,Ahmet Soylemezoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VCTP（车辆条件地形规划），通过在八个朝向评估共享的高程与地表ID图层，将车身几何约束与载重轮接触建模为代价与俯仰/侧倾预测，并规划前进/后退路线；在观测变化时选择性重算受影响查询。实验显示轮接触采样较车辆中心采样降低39.3%建模地表代价且路径更短，选择性更新在474次比较中与全量重算一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆朝向同时影响轮胎下方的地表以及车身的俯仰与侧倾，但常规地形规划往往忽略了这种车辆-地形耦合关系。作者希望保留这些关系，使路径规划能反映轮接触位置与车身姿态对可行性和代价的真实影响，并明确何时轮接触放置与朝向会影响路线选择。

Method: 提出VCTP：在八个朝向下评估共享的高程与地表ID图层；用车身几何约束判定可行性，用载重轮接触确定建模地表代价并预测俯仰和侧倾；结合D* Lite与车辆状态搜索，规划含前进和后退运动的路线；当观测改变时，重算所有受影响的车身或接触查询（支持选择性更新）。

Result: 在完全观测的两轨仿真中，在轮接触处采样相比在车辆中心采样，建模地表代价降低39.3%，同时路径更短。在RGator记录的离线规划中，VCTP相对以距离为中心的规划降低了已识别地表与已观测支撑上的建模代价，但不完整覆盖使全路线排序未能确定。在474次比较中，使用记录地图变化的选择性更新与全量重算结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明轮接触放置与车辆朝向确实会影响路线选择，VCTP能通过保留车辆-地形耦合关系改善建模代价与路径质量，且选择性更新可高效替代全量重算；不过覆盖不完整时全路线排序仍待解决。

Abstract: A vehicle's heading affects both the surfaces beneath its tires and its pitch and roll. We present Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning (VCTP), which retains these relationships by evaluating shared elevation and surface-ID layers at eight headings. Body geometry constrains admissibility, while loaded wheel contacts determine modeled surface cost and predicted pitch and roll. Established D* Lite and vehicle-state search use these evaluations to plan routes with forward and reverse motion. When observations change, VCTP recomputes every affected body or contact query. In fully observed two-track simulations, sampling at wheel contacts rather than at the vehicle center lowers modeled surface cost by 39.3% while shortening the route. In offline planning on RGator recordings, VCTP also reduces modeled costs over identified surfaces and observed support relative to distance-focused planning, although incomplete coverage leaves full-route rankings unresolved. Selective updates match full recomputation in all 474 comparisons using recorded map changes. These results identify when wheel-contact placement and vehicle heading affect route choice.

</details>


### [113] [Timely Activation of Safety Filters via One-Step Reachability Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17904)
*Javier Borquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对离散时间执行下安全滤波器可能失效的问题，提出基于一步扩展BRT的安全滤波器，保证离散执行下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Hamilton-Jacobi可达性的最小限制安全滤波器依赖连续时间假设，但实际机器人系统在离散采样间隔执行控制，导致系统可能在更新之间跳入不安全BRT，产生理论可避免却实际发生的失效。

Method: 提出一步扩展BRT，预测单步内能到达真实BRT的所有状态，将扩展边界作为安全滤波器的激活条件，使安全干预足够提前；并将扩展集建模为修改后的可达性问题，用标准连续时间求解器计算。

Result: 安全干预足够提前，从而在离散时间执行下保证正确性；扩展集可通过标准连续时间求解器计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为离散时间执行下的安全滤波器提供了一种有原则的解决方案，弥合了连续时间理论与实际离散采样之间的差距。

Abstract: Least-restrictive safety filters based on Hamilton-Jacobi reachability provide strong safety guarantees by overriding a nominal controller only when the system reaches the boundary of the set of unsafe states defined as a Backward Reachable Tube (BRT). These guarantees, however, rely on the continuous-time nature of the underlying formulation. In practice, robotic systems apply control at discrete sampling intervals, which creates a mismatch where the system may jump into the unsafe BRT between updates, allowing failures that are theoretically avoidable. This work introduces a principled solution based on a one-step expanded BRT that predicts all states capable of reaching the true BRT within a single timestep. By using this expanded boundary as the activation condition for the safety filter, safety interventions occur early enough to ensure correctness under discrete-time execution. We formulate this expanded set as a modified reachability problem and compute it using standard continuous-time solvers.

</details>


### [114] [Map2Route: Benchmarking Compositional Language-Grounded Route Planning over Semantic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17910)
*Muyi Bao,Hang Xu,Jingfan Tang,Zihan Liu,Yuxin Cai,Chen Lv,Wenshan Wang,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Map2Route基准与Grounding2Route方法，用于基于语义地图的复杂语言路线规划任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在组合式语言路线规划方面测试不充分，尤其缺乏对关系/比较/嵌套描述、有序必经区域、避让要求和多类软偏好等约束的系统评测。

Method: 构建Map2Route基准：覆盖40个场景、1000个episode，指令包含关系/比较/嵌套描述，并指定有序必经区域、避让要求、五类软偏好及空间与路线阶段范围；提出Grounding2Route，结合可执行代码式grounding、验证引导修复与范围感知规划。

Result: 在七个代表性适配基线上，Grounding2Route在所有指标上显著优于现有方法，但仍与人类示范存在较大差距。

Conclusion: Map2Route具有较高难度，任务仍有较大提升空间，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We introduce Map2Route, a human-curated benchmark for compositional language-grounded route planning over pre-built semantic maps. Map2Route contains 1,000 episodes across 40 scenes, where instructions use relational, comparative, and nested descriptions to identify route-relevant objects and regions, while specifying ordered must-pass regions, must-avoid requirements, five categories of soft preferences, and spatial and route-stage scopes, which is partially tested by existing works. Alongside Map2Route, we propose Grounding2Route, which combines executable code-as-grounding with verification-guided repair and scope-aware planning.Across seven representative adapted baselines, Grounding2Route substantially outperforms existing methods in all metrics. Despite these gains, a substantial gap to human demonstrations remains, highlighting the difficulty of Map2Route and the considerable headroom for future progress. Additional qualitative results and resources are available on https://anonymous.4open.science/w/Map2Route-F05F/.

</details>


### [115] [Multi-Session Multimodal Underwater Mapping with Acoustic and Optical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17929)
*Precious Philip-Ifabiyi,Valerio Franchi,Fausto Ferreira,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因子图优化的多航次、多模态水下建图框架，联合优化轨迹、三维地标、传感器外参与航次间对齐变换，在加泰罗尼亚海岸真实数据集上显著提升地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底精确建图对海洋科学、考古与环境监测至关重要。但不同传感器（侧扫声呐与光学相机）在不同调查航次采集的数据难以融合，主要受定位漂移和传感器偏移影响，跨航次数据配准困难。

Method: 采用因子图优化框架，联合优化载体轨迹、三维地标位置、传感器外参以及每航次的全局对齐变换；通过结合刚性的航次间校正与局部轨迹形变，同时补偿航次间偏移和航次内因导航误差累积产生的畸变。

Result: 在加泰罗尼亚海岸真实数据集上验证，所有指标（含像素精度与平均交并比）均优于未优化与刚性对齐基线；像素精度较未优化基线提升3.4%，相当于约14700 m² 区域的语义标注改善；定性结果显示在存在显著轨迹畸变和航次间错位时，声呐与光学地图仍保持一致配准。

Conclusion: 该框架能够从异构水下调查数据生成连贯的多模态海底地图，展现了在多航次、多传感器水下建图中的良好应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate seafloor mapping is essential for marine science, archaeology, and environmental monitoring. However, integrating data from different sensors, such as side-scan sonar and optical cameras, collected across separate survey sessions, remains challenging due to positioning drift and sensor offsets. This paper presents a multi-session, multimodal underwater mapping framework based on factor graph optimization. The method jointly optimizes vehicle trajectories, 3D landmark positions, sensor extrinsics, and per-session global alignment transformations. By combining rigid inter-session corrections with local trajectory deformations, it compensates for both inter-session offsets and intra-session distortions from accumulated navigation errors. The proposed methodology was validated on real-world datasets collected along the Catalan coast. Results show measurable improvements in map consistency over both unoptimized and rigid-alignment baselines across all metrics, including Pixel Accuracy and mean Intersection over Union. The method achieves a 3.4% improvement in pixel accuracy over the unoptimized baseline, corresponding to improved semantic labelling across approximately 14700 $\text{m}^2$ of mapped area. Qualitative results further show consistent co-registration between sonar and optical maps, even in the presence of significant trajectory distortions and inter-session misalignments. These findings demonstrate the potential of the proposed framework to generate coherent multimodal seafloor maps from heterogeneous underwater surveys.

</details>


### [116] [Feedback-Modulated Harmonic Policies for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17946)
*Yixuan Jia,Steven Roche,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种以命令条件傅里叶级数表示每条关节轨迹、并用状态反馈在线修正的四足运动控制方法，并在仿真对比MLP策略，实机验证高速与负载能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见学习型四足运动策略直接将观测映射到关节动作，使运动的周期性结构隐含在策略内部，缺乏显式可解释的频率表征。作者希望探索显式频率结构的替代表示。

Method: 将每条关节轨迹表示为命令条件傅里叶级数，并由上下文网络生成傅里叶系数及逐步反馈网络权重；反馈网络输出在执行中调整关节偏置、谐波增益、频率与相位。仿真中对比显式频率结构与直接输出关节目标的MLP策略，并对MLP rollout做动态模式分解分析。

Result: 谐波波形随指令速度改变频率与形状；对选定MLP rollout的动态模式分解显示主导激活模态接近足高振荡频率及其二次谐波，说明无显式傅里叶生成器也存在周期结构。Unitree Go2上，仿真训练的谐波控制器录得约3.67 m/s的板载估计峰值速度，并可在单独试验中承载最高约5.883 kg负载。

Conclusion: 显式傅里叶表示可提供可解释且有效的周期结构，实现高速与负载能力；同时表明直接关节输出策略内部也隐式习得类似周期模态。作者将其定位为初步（provisional）结果。

Abstract: Learned quadruped locomotion policies commonly map observations directly to joint-level actions, leaving the periodic structure of locomotion implicit in the policy. We investigate an alternative representation in which each joint trajectory is expressed as a command-conditioned Fourier series and modified online using feedback from the robot state. A context network generates the Fourier coefficients and the weights of a per-step feedback network, whose outputs adjust joint offsets, harmonic gains, frequency, and phase during execution. In simulation, we examine this explicit frequency structure alongside the hidden activations of an MLP policy that directly outputs joint targets. The harmonic waveforms change frequency and shape with commanded speed. Dynamic mode decomposition of selected MLP rollouts reveals dominant activation modes near the foot-height oscillation frequency and its second harmonic, showing periodic structure without an explicit Fourier generator. On a Unitree Go2, the simulation-trained harmonic controller records a provisional onboard-estimated peak speed of 3.67 meter per second and carries added loads up to 5.883 kilogram in separate trials.

</details>


### [117] [Causal-History Test-Time Scaling for Failure Recovery in Autoregressive World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18016)
*Lin Li,Long Chen,Kwunhang,Wong,Jiaming Lei,Song Jin,Shucheng Du,Chuhan Zhang,Songchen Ma,Weihao Zhang,Jun Xiao,Kwang-Ting,Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的框架，将机器人操作中的失败恢复重新表述为对因果历史的测试时扩展问题，通过三个阶段实现恢复并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）主要基于成功轨迹训练，当真实执行偏离学习到的动态时容易失败；在自回归WAMs中，执行误差会进入因果历史并持续影响后续预测，导致问题加剧。

Method: 该框架将恢复分解为三个耦合决策：何时修订因果历史、从哪里恢复可靠的历史前缀、以及哪种历史配置最支持后续执行。通过三阶段实现：1）进度感知恢复触发器检测持续无进展并在允许干预时触发恢复；2）历史前缀恢复识别不可靠的历史后缀，检索与当前物理状态匹配的历史锚点，并在保留前缀的基础上结合最新真实观测重建因果KV状态；3）假设验证比较完整历史、恢复前缀和完全重置三种假设诱导的未来延续，并采纳最佳支持假设。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界操作环境中的实验表明，任务成功率获得一致提升；消融实验证实了每个恢复阶段的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了将失败恢复视为因果历史的测试时扩展这一新视角的有效性，且无需额外训练即可在模拟与真实场景中稳定改善机器人操作性能。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and robot actions. However, existing WAMs are trained predominantly on successful trajectories, making them prone to failure when real-world execution diverges from the learned dynamics. This issue is amplified in autoregressive WAMs, where execution errors become part of the causal history and continue to influence subsequent predictions. To this end, we introduce \method{}, a training-free framework that reformulates failure recovery as \emph{test-time scaling over causal histories}. This formulation decomposes recovery into three coupled decisions: \emph{when} to revise the causal history, \emph{where} to recover a reliable history prefix, and \emph{which} history configuration best supports subsequent execution. Specifically, \method{} realizes these decisions through three stages: 1) \textbf{Progress-Aware Recovery Trigger} detects persistent non-progress and triggers recovery only when the current execution state permits intervention; 2) \textbf{History-Prefix Recovery} identifies the unreliable history suffix, retrieves a historical anchor matching the current physical state, and reconstructs the causal KV state from the retained prefix while conditioning on the latest real observation; and 3) \textbf{Hypothesis Verification} compares the future continuations induced by complete-history, recovered-prefix, and full-reset hypotheses, and commits the best-supported hypothesis. Experiments in both simulated and real-world manipulation settings demonstrate consistent improvements in task success, while ablations confirm the contribution of each recovery stage.

</details>


### [118] [Online Multimodal Workload Assessment in Contact-Rich Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18031)
*Yanyi Chen,Fan Yang,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线多模态交互负荷评估框架，融合力/运动学/皮电信号构建四个可解释负荷因子，并利用路径曲率与任务进程动态调整权重，在24人实验中与瞳孔直径参考达到ρ30=0.308，性能媲美主流学习方法且更具透明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的物理人机交互（pHRI）对物理交互、运动调节和生理响应提出时变需求，因此需要连续评估交互负荷；现有方法多为黑箱或静态权重，缺乏可解释性与动态适应性。

Method: 构建在线多模态评估框架，将交互力（wrench）、平面工具中心点（TCP）运动学和皮肤电导水平（SCL）整合为四个可解释的负荷相关因子；用路径曲率反映运动需求变化、用任务进程刻画生理随时间漂移来调整各因子相对贡献。在24名被试、18种温度/噪声/照度组合、两种导纳控制模式下评估，采用严格LOSO，以标准化瞳孔直径PD_z为独立生理参考，并与静态变体及代表性学习基线比较。

Result: 30秒分块Spearman相关为ρ30=0.308，24名被试中23名呈正向个体对应；整体性能与最优学习基线相当，优于对应固定权重版本，并在两种导纳控制模式下表现一致。

Conclusion: 该框架为接触丰富pHRI中的连续交互负荷评估提供了一条透明、可解释的技术路径，在保持竞争力的同时提升了可解释性与稳定性。

Abstract: Contact-rich physical human--robot interaction (pHRI) imposes time-varying demands associated with physical interaction, motor regulation, and physiological response, motivating continuous assessment of interaction workload. This paper presents an online multimodal assessment framework that integrates interaction wrench, planar tool-center-point (TCP) kinematics, and skin conductance level (SCL) into four interpretable workload-related factors. Their relative contributions are adjusted using path curvature to reflect changes in motion demand and task progression to account for gradual physiological variation over time. The framework was evaluated with 24 participants across 18 controlled combinations of temperature, acoustic noise, and illuminance under two admittance-control modes. Strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation used standardized pupil diameter ($\mathrm{PD}_z$) as an independent physiological reference and included comparisons with static variants and representative state-of-the-art learning-based baselines. The proposed framework achieves a cohort-mean $30\,\mathrm{s}$ block-wise Spearman correlation of $ρ_{30}=0.308$ with the physiological reference, with positive subject-level correspondence in 23 of 24 participants. Its overall performance is comparable to the state-of-the-art learning-based baseline. At the same time, our framework keeps the assessment process transparent through explicit workload-related factors and defined weighting rules, while outperforming the corresponding fixed-weight formulation. The framework also maintains consistent performance across the two tested admittance-control modes. These results support a transparent and interpretable approach to continuous interaction workload assessment in contact-rich pHRI.

</details>


### [119] [Geometric Shortcuts for Complex Trunk Postures: Dual-Helicity Coupling Enables Low-Dimensional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18050)
*Huishi Huang,Danlu Chen,Matteo Lo Preti,Jun Liu,Marcelo H. Ang,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出象鼻复杂姿态部分源于对侧斜肌之间"双螺旋耦合"的低维几何捷径，并通过对软体机器人原型的验证，仅用两个几何参数即可生成丰富的象鼻式姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 象鼻能够产生高度复杂的姿态，但若仅依赖精细的分段肌肉激活来实现这种复杂性，控制代价过高。作者希望探究是否存在更简洁的机制来源，并借此为连续体机器人设计提供思路及相关生物学假说。

Method: 提出"双螺旋耦合"假说：对侧手性的斜向肌纤维相互耦合，构成一种低维几何捷径。在此基础上构建简化的软体机器人原型，仅改变两个几何参数进行姿态生成实验。

Result: 在原型中，仅调节两个几何参数即可产生一个涵盖广泛象鼻式姿态的"姿态库"，说明复杂姿态可在低维控制下实现。

Conclusion: 象鼻复杂的姿态能力可部分归因于几何层面的低维机制，而非完全依赖精细分段激活；这暗示存在一种双层控制架构，并对象鼻生物学假说及连续体机器人设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: How do elephant trunks generate complex postures without relying solely on fine segmental activation? We propose that part of this complexity arises from a low-dimensional geometric shortcut: dual-helicity coupling between opposite-handed oblique muscles. In a simplified soft-robotic prototype, varying only two geometric parameters generates a broad library of elephant-like postures, suggesting a dual-layer control architecture with implications for continuum robot design and biological hypotheses.

</details>


### [120] [CLASP: A Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking Robot with a Soft Rolling-Band Gripper for Fresh-Market Blueberry Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18051)
*Yixuan Xia,Yilin Cai,Natalia Belen Espinoza,Changying Li,Zilfina Rubio Ames,Xin Zhang,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CLASP——一种簇级自主选择性采摘机器人，配备软体主动滚带夹爪（SARB-Gripper），通过双柔性带包络浆果簇并滚动将成熟果实依次摘下，配合全局-局部感知流水线实现蓝莓簇级选择性采收，野外试验抓取成功率达92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲜食蓝莓需选择性强且轻柔的采摘，人工成本高；跨行机械采收器速度快但非选择性，会损伤混熟果实并把产量限制在加工市场；现有选择性机器人多针对单果而非果簇，对果小且密集成簇的蓝莓采摘效率低。因此需要一种簇级的选择性采摘方案。

Method: 提出CLASP机器人及软体主动滚带夹爪（SARB-Gripper）：两条柔性带包络果簇并相对果实滚动，依次摘下成熟果实，同时闭环调节拉力使施加载荷低于未成熟果实的脱落阈值。感知采用全局-局部流水线，由眼对手相机完成全局果簇检测与目标选择，眼在手上相机完成局部定位与果簇朝向估计。

Result: 田间测量表明成熟与未成熟果实之间存在清晰的脱落力分离；SARB-Gripper的拉力跟踪误差在3.7%以内，可实现簇级选择性采收。端到端田间试验中，CLASP自主抓取25个果簇中的23个（92%）。单台组件成本约3326美元。

Conclusion: CLASP为鲜食蓝莓提供了一种可扩展的簇级选择性采收方案，兼顾选择性与效率，并具备较低的单位成本。

Abstract: Fresh-market blueberries require selective, gentle picking, which is labor-intensive and expensive. Over-the-row machine harvesters are fast but non-selective, bruising mixed-ripeness fruit and limiting yield to the processing market. Selective robotic harvesters typically target individual fruits rather than fruit clusters, which limits harvesting efficiency for small, densely clustered blueberries. This paper presents CLASP, a Cluster-Level Autonomous Selective Picking robot with a Soft Active Rolling-Band Gripper (SARB-Gripper). Two compliant bands envelop the cluster and roll against the fruit, drawing mature berries off in sequence, while closed-loop regulation of the pulling force keeps the applied load below the immature detachment threshold. A global-to-local perception pipeline pairs an eye-to-hand camera for global cluster detection and target selection with an eye-in-hand camera for local localization and cluster orientation estimation. Field measurements confirm a clear detachment-force separation between mature and immature fruit, and the SARB-Gripper reproduces a commanded pulling force to within \SI{3.7}{\percent}, enabling selective harvesting at the cluster level. In end-to-end field trials, CLASP autonomously grasped 23 of 25 presented clusters (\SI{92}{\percent}). With the component cost of approximately \$3326 per unit, CLASP offers a scalable approach to selective cluster-level harvesting for fresh-market blueberries.

</details>


### [121] [An Efficient Algorithm for Minimum-Pressure Growth Planning of Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18070)
*Andres C. Torres,Tobia Marcucci,Elliot W. Hawkes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究藤蔓机器人围绕多面体障碍物生长时的最小压力路径规划问题，提出一种高效算法，在2D可保证全局最优，在3D可给出近似最优解，误差随离散化参数趋零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人靠尖端延伸在杂乱环境中运动，但其生长规划（寻找最优生长路径）研究较少；现有规划器未考虑跟随路径所需生长压力，压力过高会导致机器人爆裂。

Method: 首先推导任意形状藤蔓机器人的生长压力方程，证明总存在一条分段线性且仅在障碍物特定点弯曲的最小压力路径；据此将生长规划问题化简为带时变权的最短路问题，并用改进的Dijkstra算法高效求解。

Result: 数值仿真验证了方法的速度与可扩展性，硬件实验验证了算法有效性，并提供了开源高性能Python包VinePlanner。

Conclusion: 该工作为藤蔓机器人提供了考虑生长压力的最优路径规划方法，2D保证全局最优、3D近似最优，并可实际部署。

Abstract: Vine robots navigate cluttered environments by extending from their tip. Although their ability to operate in such environments has been extensively demonstrated, little work has addressed growth planning, i.e., finding optimal growth paths. Moreover, existing planners do not account for the growth pressure necessary to follow a given path, which can cause the robot to burst when it is too high. In this paper, we address the problem of finding minimum-pressure paths for vine robots growing around polytopic obstacles. We propose an efficient algorithm that is guaranteed to find globally optimal solutions in 2D and approximate solutions in 3D, with an error that vanishes as a discretization parameter approaches zero. First, we derive a growth pressure equation for vine robots of arbitrary shape, which we use to show that there always exists a minimum-pressure path that is piecewise-linear and can bend only at specific points on the obstacles. We then leverage this observation to reduce the growth-planning problem to a shortest-path problem with time-dependent weights, which we efficiently solve using a modified Dijkstra's algorithm. We demonstrate the speed and scalability of our approach through numerical simulations. We also validate our algorithm with hardware experiments and provide an open-source and high-performance implementation in the Python package, VinePlanner: https://github.com/Ahsoka/VinePlanner.

</details>


### [122] [Characterizing Refraction-Induced Ranging Bias in Underwater Collaborative Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18073)
*Timothy Kogucki,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究折射引起的声学测距偏差对多智能体协同定位在公里尺度上的影响，通过仿真实验揭示其在声速梯度剧烈区域会造成显著的轨迹估计退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体测距辅助导航是大尺度水下定位的有前景方案，但其精度高度依赖测距质量。声速变化会导致声线折射弯曲，而标准传感器融合流程为算法简便假设直线传播，这种折射会系统性地使测距结果偏大。然而，该偏差在公里尺度多智能体协同定位中的影响尚未被探索。

Method: 作者开展了一系列公里尺度的多智能体仿真实验：使用HYCOM再分析数据重建真实海洋环境，通过射线追踪生成考虑折射的测距值，并采用集中式多智能体因子图估计器量化由此产生的测量偏差对轨迹估计的影响。

Result: 初步结果表明，折射引起的偏差可导致估计轨迹显著退化，尤其是在声速梯度剧烈的区域。

Conclusion: 折射偏差对公里尺度多智能体协同定位的影响不可忽视，且在强声速梯度区域尤为严重。作者还开源了仿真环境以支持后续研究。

Abstract: This work studies how refraction-induced bias on acoustic ranging affects multi-agent collaborative localization in a range of oceanographic conditions and spatial scales. While multi-agent range-aided navigation, which uses range measurements to either fixed infrastructure or other agents, is a promising solution to the challenges of large-scale underwater localization, its accuracy depends strongly on the quality of range measurements. Sound speed variability induces refraction (bending) of acoustic rays, yet, for algorithmic tractability, standard sensor fusion pipelines assume straight-line propagation. This refraction systematically biases range measurements to be longer than the straight-line assumption predicts. However, the effects of this bias on multi-agent collaborative localization on kilometer scales remains unexplored. We present a series of simulated experiments with several agents operating over kilometer scales. The simulation uses HYCOM reanalysis data to recreate realistic oceanographic conditions, ray tracing to generate refraction-informed ranges, and a centralized multi-agent factor graph estimator to quantify the resulting measurement bias on estimated trajectories. Preliminary results indicate that refraction-induced bias can induce significant degradation of estimated trajectories, particularly in regions with sharp sound-speed gradients. We also share the simulation environment to support further studies https://github.com/UMich-RobotExploration/manta-ray.

</details>


### [123] [Not All Layers Need Tuning: Diagnosing and Directing Adaptation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18084)
*Shahram Najam Syed,Arthur Jakobsson,Prayuj Sachdev,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型微调成本高,且各网络区域对适应需求并不均等。本文在五种架构上测量得到"适应光谱",并提出观测-诊断-分配-适配流水线,用无标注目标观测预估各区域适应成本,做变秩LoRA分配并在LIBERO、CALVIN及真实xArm-7上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA微调方法对每个网络区域施加同等容量的适配器,默认各区域需要等量调整,这一假设缺乏检验。而针对新部署环境进行全量微调代价高昂,因此需要一种能在微调开始前就识别"哪里需要改、改多少"的方法。

Method: 在五种架构差异显著的VLA(OpenVLA-OFT、π0、SmolVLA、DTP、Octo,93M-7B参数)上用"区域隔离微调下的归一化参数位移"度量各区域适应成本。基于此提出四步流水线:观测(10个无标注目标样本)、诊断(融合无参考梯度信号、Monte Carlo Dropout信号与对缓存源参考的CKA分数,估计各区域成本)、分配(在参数预算下转换为变秩LoRA适配器并冻结已良好校准区域)、适配(标准LoRA微调)。

Result: 诊断的区域排序中位Spearman为0.91;在LIBERO和CALVIN上,分配策略在所有测试预算下均匹配或超过均匀LoRA。真实xArm-7上,在指令措辞变化场景中仅用0.04%可训练参数即匹配全量微调;在五个未重训的留出场景中优于所有基线,30次rollout中成功11-23次,而最强参数高效基线在同等或更大预算下为8-18次,全量微调为2-11次。

Conclusion: VLA中的适应成本具有足够结构化的规律,可以在微调开始之前被测量和利用,从而以极小的可训练参数代价获得优于均匀适配与全量微调的泛化表现。}

Abstract: Fine-tuning a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model for a new deployment environment is expensive, yet most methods apply uniform-capacity adapters to every network region as if every region requires equal adjustment. This paper tests that assumption on five architecturally diverse VLAs (OpenVLA-OFT, $π_0$, SmolVLA, DTP, Octo; 93M-7B parameters). Measuring per-region adaptation cost as normalized parameter displacement under region-isolated fine-tuning reveals an adaptation spectrum in which appearance shifts concentrate cost in the vision encoder, instruction shifts in the language backbone, and novel-object shifts in the vision encoder together with the action head, across all five architectures. To exploit this structure, we introduce a pipeline that observes, diagnoses, allocates, and adapts. From ten unlabeled target observations and without fine-tuning, the diagnostic estimates per-region cost by combining reference-free gradient and Monte Carlo Dropout signals with a Centered Kernel Alignment score against a cached source reference; the allocator converts the estimates into variable-rank LoRA adapters under a parameter budget and freezes well-calibrated regions; and standard LoRA fine-tuning trains the resulting adapters. The diagnostic ranks regions within each deployment at a median Spearman of 0.91, and the allocation matches or exceeds uniform LoRA at every budget we tested on LIBERO and CALVIN. On a physical xArm-7, the pipeline matches full fine-tuning under an instruction-wording shift with 0.04% of its trainable parameters, and on five held-out scenes evaluated without retraining it leads every baseline, with 11-23 successes of 30 rollouts against 8-18 for the strongest parameter-efficient baseline at equal or larger budgets and 2-11 for full fine-tuning. These results suggest that adaptation cost in VLAs is structured enough to measure before fine-tuning begins.

</details>


### [124] [ReRadar: Robust Radar Global Localization via Rotation-Equivariant Descriptor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18092)
*Duc Manh Nguyen,Truong Giang Dao,Gia Nghiem Luong,Viet Trung Hoang,Anh Quang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReRadar：基于旋转等变特征与NetVLAD聚合的毫米波雷达全局定位流水线，结合描述子检索与地标匹配实现3-DoF位姿估计，在多个数据集上达到高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描式毫米波雷达的全局定位仍具挑战性，因为现有的场景识别描述子往往丢弃了精确位姿检索所需的（旋转相关）空间结构信息。

Method: 使用可导向卷积神经网络（steerable CNN）提取旋转等变的中间特征；通过群池化（group pooling）与NetVLAD聚合形成旋转不变描述子；将描述子检索与基于地标的匹配相结合，估计机器人3自由度（3-DoF）位姿。

Result: 在固定数据库-查询评估中：经目标数据集适配后，OORD Bellmouth达99.37% Recall@1，Mulran DCC01达91.44% Recall@1、F1_max 80.99%，falling-snow Boreas序列达99.38% Recall@1；无目标数据集数据时，跨数据集模型在OORD达98.07% Recall@1，与所评估的SOTA方法性能相当。

Conclusion: ReRadar通过保留旋转等变结构的雷达描述子并融合检索与地标匹配，实现了高精度的毫米波雷达全局定位，且在数据适配与跨数据集场景下均具竞争力。

Abstract: Global localization with scanning millimeter-wave radar remains challenging because place-recognition descriptors often discard spatial structure needed for accurate pose retrieval. We present ReRadar, a radar global localization pipeline that extracts rotation-equivariant intermediate features using steerable convolutional neural networks, forms rotation-invariant descriptors through group pooling and NetVLAD aggregation, and combines descriptor retrieval with landmark-based matching to estimate the robot's three-degree-of-freedom (3-DoF) pose. Across fixed database-query evaluations, ReRadar with target-dataset adaptation achieves 99.37% Recall@1 on OORD Bellmouth, 91.44% Recall@1 with 80.99% F1_max on Mulran DCC01, and 99.38% Recall@1 on falling-snow Boreas sequence. Without target-dataset data, the cross-dataset model reaches 98.07% Recall@1 on OORD, performing comparably to the evaluated state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Pixel Similarity: Task-Aware Evaluation of GAN-Based Synthetic Sonar Data for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18100)
*Hannan Ejaz Keen,Muhammad Moazam Fraz,Karsten Berns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究GAN生成合成声呐图像中，传统图像保真度指标（SSIM、PSNR、MSE）是否能反映下游感知性能，发现二者存在明显不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可降低机器人感知中数据采集与标注成本，但生成能保留下游感知相关特征的传感器观测仍具挑战，尤其对声呐图像。现有评价多依赖图像保真度指标，其能否代表下游感知性能尚不明确。

Method: 采用Pix2Pix条件GAN，配置四种不同感受野的判别器（PixelGAN、PatchGAN-16、PatchGAN-70、ImageGAN），基于两个声呐数据集训练。用SSIM、PSNR、MSE等像素级保真度指标评估，并引入任务导向评估：用真实声呐图像训练YOLOX-S、YOLOX-L和Faster R-CNN检测器，在各配置生成的图像上以相同测试样本与标注进行检测评估。

Result: 图像保真度与下游目标检测性能存在偏差：SSIM、PSNR、MSE最优的配置并未始终取得最佳检测性能。PatchGAN配置虽未获得最高像素级相似度分数，却取得了较强的下游检测结果。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的数据集与模型范围内，仅凭像素级图像保真度指标可能无法一致地刻画合成声呐观测中与任务相关的真实感，因此建议对面向机器人感知的合成传感器数据采用任务感知评估。

Abstract: Synthetic data can reduce the cost of collecting and annotating training data for robotic perception, but generating sensor observations that preserve the characteristics relevant to downstream perception remains challenging, particularly for sonar imagery. In this work, we investigate whether conventional image-fidelity metrics adequately reflect the downstream perception performance of GAN-generated synthetic sonar data. We employ a Pix2Pix conditional generative adversarial network with four discriminator configurations characterized by different receptive fields: PixelGAN, PatchGAN-16, PatchGAN-70, and ImageGAN. The models are trained using sonar imagery from two datasets and evaluated using conventional image-fidelity metrics, including Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and Mean Squared Error (MSE). To complement these pixel-level measures with task-oriented evaluation, YOLOX-S, YOLOX-L, and Faster R-CNN detectors are trained exclusively on real sonar imagery and subsequently evaluated on the GAN-generated images using identical test samples and annotations across all discriminator configurations. The results reveal a discrepancy between image-fidelity and downstream object-detection performance: the configuration achieving the best SSIM, PSNR, and MSE does not consistently yield the best detection performance. In particular, PatchGAN configurations achieve strong downstream detection results despite not achieving the highest pixel-level similarity scores. These findings suggest, for the datasets and models considered, pixel-level image-fidelity metrics alone may not consistently capture the task-relevant realism of synthetic sonar observations and motivate the use of task-aware evaluation for synthetic sensor data intended for robotic perception.

</details>


### [126] [Technical Report: One-Step Drifting Action Heads for GR00T N1.7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18108)
*Xihe Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将GR00T N1.7的迭代扩散动作头替换为一步drift动作头可显著降低推理耗时，但在LIBERO上带来系统性任务成功率下降，属速度—成功率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步动作生成能大幅削减VLA策略的推理成本，但其对闭环任务成功率的影响尚不明确，本文对此进行技术性考察。

Method: 在GR00T N1.7变体中，用一步drifting动作头替换迭代diffusion-transformer动作头，并加入面向异步chunk替换的overlap条件扩展；多随机种子drifting实验在两块NVIDIA A800上训练，并在LIBERO上评估。

Result: 动作头平均前向时间由约45.3ms降至5.0ms，backbone加动作头总时间由约70.0ms降至30.6ms；但成功率系统性下降：LIBERO-Spatial 64.0±4.0%，LIBERO-Goal 52.0±1.0%，LIBERO-Long 26.0±2.6%，种子方差小。

Conclusion: 作者将该结果报告为速度—成功率权衡而非整体改进，并讨论了确定性一步模式平均、批次相关几何估计、长开环chunk执行以及同步LIBERO评估未使用异步overlap路径等潜在原因。

Abstract: One-step action generation can substantially reduce the inference cost of vision-language-action (VLA) policies, but its effect on closed-loop task success remains an open question. This technical report studies a GR00T N1.7 variant in which the iterative diffusion-transformer action head is replaced by a one-step drifting action head, together with an overlap-conditioned extension for asynchronous chunk replacement. All multi-seed drifting runs were trained on two NVIDIA A800 GPUs. On LIBERO, the action head reduces the mean model-forward time of the action head from approximately $45.3\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $5.0\,\mathrm{ms}$, while the measured backbone-plus-head time falls from approximately $70.0\,\mathrm{ms}$ to $30.6\,\mathrm{ms}$. However, this speedup is accompanied by a systematic reduction in task success. Across three drifting seeds, success is $64.0\pm4.0\%$ on LIBERO-Spatial, $52.0\pm1.0\%$ on LIBERO-Goal, and $26.0\pm2.6\%$ on LIBERO-Long. The low seed variance indicates that the degradation is not explained by random initialization alone. We report the result as a speed--success trade-off rather than an overall improvement, and discuss likely contributing factors including deterministic one-step mode averaging, batch-dependent geometry estimation, long open-loop chunk execution, and the fact that synchronous LIBERO evaluation does not exercise the asynchronous overlap path.

</details>


### [127] [A Comprehensive Review of Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18111)
*Satyam Gaba,Krutiksinh Rana,Siva Sai,Vinay Chamola,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于生成式物理人工智能（GPAI）的系统性综述，提出五类方法分类学并分析其架构、应用与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型与物理实体结合推动机器人学发展，形成能自主感知、推理和行动的GPAI系统，但其架构基础、应用现状与关键局限尚缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 提出五类分类学：机器人基础模型（RFM）实现跨平台技能迁移；视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）实现端到端多模态感知控制；大行为模型（LBM）生成类人运动；扩散策略模型（DPM）生成时间连贯动作；世界基础模型（WFM）做物理合规仿真与数据生成。并分析五者的互补关系。

Result: 在自动驾驶、工业自动化、医疗机器人和人形系统等场景中，各方法均展现出显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 总结了数据高效学习、仿真到现实迁移、边缘兼容架构和安全框架等有前景的研究方向，推动面向IoT互联环境的具身AI发展。

Abstract: The integration of large-scale foundation models with physical embodiments has led to significant advancements in robotics known as Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence (GPAI). These agentic AI systems autonomously perceive, reason, and act in complex real-world situations. This survey comprehensively analyzes GPAI systems, focusing on their architectural foundations, current applications, and key limitations. We introduce a taxonomy of five distinct approaches: Robot Foundation Models (RFMs) for cross-platform skill transfer; Vision-Language Action (VLA) models for end-to-end multi-modal perception and control; Large Behavior Models (LBMs) for human-like movement generation; Diffusion Policy Models (DPMs) for diffusion model-based temporally coherent action generation; and World Foundation Models (WFMs) for physics-compliant simulation and data generation. We examine how these approaches complement each other: WFMs generate training data for VLAs and DPMs, RFMs enable cross-platform deployment of learned policies, while LBMs provide motion priors for natural behavior. Through examples across autonomous vehicles, industrial automation, healthcare robotics, and humanoid systems, we identify significant performance improvements and summarize promising research directions in data-efficient learning, sim-to-real transfer, edge-compatible architectures, and safety frameworks. These insights advance embodied AI for IoT-connected environments where intelligent agents interact with networked sensors, actuators, and edge devices.

</details>


### [128] [OpenDexGrasp: Open-vocabulary Task-Oriented Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18117)
*Jiyao Zhang,Junhan Wang,Tianyu Wang,Zeyuan Chen,Anthony Bolten,Yitong Peng,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OpenDexGrasp，一个面向开放词汇任务导向灵巧抓取生成的数据与生成建模框架，结合大规模合成与人类遥操作对齐数据，学习共享感知-动作潜在表示，直接生成任务一致的可执行高自由度抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取合成虽能生成稳定、物理合理的姿态，但真实操作需要保持任务所隐含的功能。开放词汇任务导向抓取要求机器人从自由语言中推断功能意图，并将其落地到多视角视觉观测与物体几何上，生成可执行的高自由度抓取。

Method: OpenDexGrasp框架包含两个部分：OpenDexVerse按Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) 配方提供双源监督，其中OpenDex-Scale通过自动抓取合成与视觉-语言标注提供大规模语义和几何覆盖，OpenDex-Align通过人类遥操作和类别级迁移提供高质量具身对齐；OpenDexGrasp学习共享感知-动作潜在表示，将开放词汇视觉-语言上下文与灵巧动作生成耦合，通过可供性定位和抓取生成在该潜在空间上提供互补监督，无需单独的可供性到姿态推断阶段。

Result: 大量仿真和真实机器人实验表明，方法在功能对齐、物理可行性、对未见类别的泛化以及真实世界执行成功率方面均有提升。

Conclusion: OpenDexGrasp统一了数据与建模，实现了开放词汇任务导向灵巧抓取的直接生成，并在功能一致性、可行性和泛化性上取得优势，更多细节与视频见项目主页。

Abstract: Dexterous grasp synthesis has advanced rapidly in generating stable and physically plausible hand poses, but real-world manipulation requires grasps that preserve the function implied by the task. We study open-vocabulary task-oriented dexterous grasp generation, where a robot must infer functional intent from free-form language, ground it in multi-view visual observations and object geometry, and generate an executable high-degree-of-freedom grasp. We present OpenDexGrasp, a unified data and generative modeling framework for this setting. OpenDexVerse provides dual-source supervision organized by the Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) Recipe: OpenDex-Scale offers large-scale semantic and geometric coverage through automatic grasp synthesis and vision-language annotation, while OpenDex-Align supplies high-quality embodied alignment through human teleoperation and category-level transfer. OpenDexGrasp learns a shared perception-action latent representation that couples open-vocabulary vision-language context with dexterous action generation. Affordance grounding and grasp generation provide complementary supervision over this latent space, enabling direct generation of task-consistent dexterous grasps without a separate affordance-to-pose inference stage. Extensive simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate improved functional alignment, physical feasibility, generalization to unseen categories, and real-world execution success. Additional details and videos are available at https://opendexgrasp.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [Fetch My Beer: Synthetic-to-real Hierarchical Policy for Smooth Pick-and-place](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18119)
*Yingyue Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Ruida Zhang,Bowen Fu,Guangyao Zhai,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出合成到真实框架，结合物理验证的数据生成与分层扩散控制器，实现液体容器稳定运输，抑制晃动与溅洒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人需要动态敏感操作，成功不仅取决于到达目标状态，还依赖执行过程中维持稳定的物体动力学。液体容器运输需抑制晃动、防止溅洒，对运动平滑性和轨迹级稳定性要求高，而现有系统存在明显不足：流体仿真在线强化学习代价过高；人类遥操作引入非预期加速度导致模仿学习中出现晃动；当前策略流程只优化任务完成而非动态稳定性。

Method: 提出合成到真实（synthetic-to-real）框架，将物理验证的数据生成与分层、基于扩散的控制器结合。可扩展数据流程合成抓取，通过视觉语言模型过滤不稳定姿态，并用流体仿真验证运输轨迹。策略分为：高层模块将语言和视觉观测转化为SE(3)控制目标；潜在扩散控制器先在紧凑潜空间中高效规划，再解码密集动作块，从而实现平滑稳定运动所需的高控制频率。

Result: 大量实验表明，该系统在运输平滑性和动态稳定性方面优于当前最先进的操作策略。

Conclusion: 该合成到真实框架通过物理验证数据生成与分层扩散控制，可有效解决液体容器稳定运输中的晃动与溅洒问题，在平滑性和动态稳定性上超越现有方法。

Abstract: Many real-world robotic applications require dynamically sensitive manipulation, where success depends not only on reaching a target state but on maintaining stable object dynamics throughout execution. We study the stable transport of liquid-filled containers, where a robot must move objects to target locations while suppressing sloshing and preventing spillage. Unlike conventional pick-and-place, this task imposes stringent requirements on motion smoothness and trajectory-level stability, exposing clear limitations in existing systems. Specifically, fluid simulation remains too costly for online reinforcement learning; human teleoperation introduces unintended accelerations that induce sloshing during imitation learning; and current policy pipelines optimize for task completion rather than dynamic stability. We propose a synthetic-to-real framework coupling physically validated data generation with a hierarchical, diffusion-based controller. The scalable data pipeline synthesizes grasps, filters unstable poses via a vision-language model, and validates transport trajectories through fluid simulation. The policy is organized with a high-level module that translates language and visual observations into SE(3) control targets, and a latent diffusion controller that first plans efficiently in a compact latent space and then decodes dense action chunks, enabling the high control frequency needed for smooth and stable motion. Extensive experiments show our system outperforms state-of-the-art manipulation policies in transport smoothness and dynamic stability. Our project page: https://fetch-my-beer.github.io/

</details>


### [130] [Prior Evolution and Task Alignment for Aerial Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18153)
*Weiliang Deng,Zhengyang Dang,Yao Mu,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在解析式轨迹优化框架中引入学习方法，通过轨迹先验、CEM进化与执行感知Critic，缓解空中抓取轨迹优化的非凸敏感与目标设计局限，提升可靠性、一致性与抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受猛禽空中捕食启发，研究者用轨迹优化复现空中抓取机动，但存在两大实践局限：一是优化问题高度非凸、对初始化敏感，在有限算力预算下难以获得高质量解；二是人工设计的数值目标只是对成功抓取的有限数学抽象，未必能完全刻画决定任务成败的因素。

Method: 在一个解析规划器内结合学习：首先从优化得到的运动轨迹中学习轨迹先验；随后通过基于CEM的过程进化该先验，用实际使用的优化器评估采样得到的初始化，并保留表现良好的初始化作为新监督数据。同时训练一个执行感知Critic（Execution-Aware Critic），从接触、抬升与完成结果中学习，判断优化后的轨迹在物理执行中是否可能成功；其冻结的能量函数可进一步作为可微抓取代价，使执行数据直接塑造轨迹生成。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，该方法在优化可靠性、轨迹一致性与抓取性能方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 将学习嵌入解析式规划器，可有效应对空中抓取轨迹优化的初始化敏感与目标抽象不足问题，并借助执行感知的代价塑造实现更贴合实际执行的成功轨迹生成。

Abstract: Aerial grasping is a remarkable capability exhibited by predatory birds, allowing them to capture prey through highly coordinated maneuvers in flight. Inspired by this capability, researchers have developed various formulations to reproduce such maneuvers through trajectory optimization. However, two limitations remain in practice. First, the resulting optimization problem is highly nonconvex and sensitive to initialization, making high-quality solutions difficult to obtain under a limited computational budget. Second, prescribed numerical objectives are human-designed abstractions that describe successful grasping through a limited set of mathematically tractable quantities and may not fully capture what determines task success. We investigate how learning can address these limitations within an analytical planner. Accordingly, a trajectory prior is first learned from optimized motions and then evolved through a CEM-based process that evaluates sampled initializations with the deployed optimizer and retains favorable ones as new supervision. An Execution-Aware Critic learns from contact, lift, and completion outcomes to assess whether the optimized trajectories are likely to succeed in physical execution. Its frozen energy can further serve as a differentiable grasping cost, allowing execution data to directly shape trajectory generation. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved optimization reliability, trajectory consistency, and grasping performance.

</details>


### [131] [Energy-Regularized Imitation Learning for Force- and Work-Aware Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18164)
*Toshiki Otani,Hiromu Taketsugu,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文把"节能操作"建模为有物理依据的学习问题，定义关节空间机械功指标，训练可微能量预测器将其转化为可微正则项，用于微调预训练操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略通常只追求任务成功率，容易产生物理上低效的运动。但仿真器中的机械功等物理量不可微，难以直接作为优化目标，而构建显式可微动力学模型成本高。因此需要一种不依赖可微动力学即可抑制低效运动的方法。

Method: 从关节力矩与角位移定义关节空间机械功代理量；训练一个可微能量预测器，依据机器人状态与动作估计该功；用该预测器把仿真器侧不可微物理量转为可微正则项，微调预训练操作策略。在 RLBench 上以 RVT-2 为基座实现，评测 12 个涵盖接触、铰接运动、放置、推动和清扫的操作任务。

Result: 微调后平均机械功从 208.8J 降至 204.4J（降低约 2.1%），同时平均任务成功率由 86.2% 小幅提升至 86.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，功感知的策略优化能在无需显式可微动力学模型的情况下抑制物理低效运动，且不牺牲甚至略微提升任务成功率。

Abstract: This paper studies energy-aware manipulation as a physically grounded learning problem. We define a joint-space mechanical-work proxy from joint torque and angular displacement, and train a differentiable energy predictor that estimates this work from robot states and actions. The predictor converts a non-differentiable simulator-side physical quantity into a differentiable regularizer for fine-tuning a pretrained manipulation policy. We instantiate the framework with RVT-2 on RLBench and evaluate 12 manipulation tasks involving object contact, articulated motion, placement, pushing, and sweeping. The proposed fine-tuning reduces the average mechanical work from 208.8J to 204.4J (i.e., 2.1% reduction), while the mean task success rate also increases slightly from 86.2% to 86.9%. These results show that work-aware policy optimization can suppress physically inefficient motion without requiring an explicit differentiable dynamics model.

</details>


### [132] [LUMO: Designing Luminous Contact Morphology for Repeatable Whole-Finger Contact Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18165)
*Dong Ho Kang,Youngsu Ko,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LUMO通过嵌入式LED照亮柔性硅胶垫，接触变形改变侧面出光模式，使接触位置可外部观测，并据此结合关节力矩估计法向力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低阻抗机器人手指仅靠关节力矩无法区分指尖小力与近关节大力，因此需要同时获知接触沿手指的位置。

Method: 设计LED与柔性硅胶垫集成的结构，使同一结构同时承载接触载荷并产生位置相关的光学响应；通过刚-软接触仿真、光线追迹和多目标贝叶斯优化截面、垫轮廓、刚性载体与侧向空隙，兼顾低预载顺应和高载荷限制变形及不同接触位置的光学分离。

Result: 在两种硅胶、六个接触位置以及10 mm和30 mm球形压头下，优化形态将相邻位置分离度相对重新建立接触的变异提高15%–59%；利用已知LED间距从光学响应估计接触位置并结合关节力矩，在931个样本上达到1.44 N法向力MAE；双指手抓取时多个连杆同时出现局部侧面响应。

Conclusion: LUMO将接触位置变为可外部观测信息，并与关节力矩融合实现较准确的法向力估计，且在多指手抓取中可提供多连杆局部接触响应。

Abstract: A low-impedance robot finger reports through joint torque how strongly it is loaded, but the same torque can arise from a small force near the fingertip or a large force near the joint. Resolving the force therefore requires knowing where along the finger contact occurred. LUMO makes that location externally observable. Embedded LEDs illuminate a compliant silicone pad, and contact deforms the pad so that light emerging from the finger's side changes in a pattern set by where the load acts. Because the same structure also carries the contact load, we optimize its cross-section, including the pad profile, rigid carrier, and lateral void, for two behaviors at once. Mechanically, the pad conforms under low preload while the carrier increasingly restricts further deformation as load rises. Optically, different contact locations produce separated responses on the finger's side. The search uses rigid--soft contact simulation, ray tracing, and multi-objective Bayesian optimization. Across two silicones, six contact locations, and 10- and 30-mm spherical indenters, the optimized morphologies improve neighboring-location separation relative to variation from re-establishing contact by \(15\)--\(59\%\). Estimating contact location from the optical response using the known LED spacing and combining it with joint torque gives \(1.44~\mathrm{N}\) normal-force MAE over 931 samples. In a two-finger hand, localized side responses appear on several links simultaneously during grasps.

</details>


### [133] [Characterizing Replay Retention Under Dynamics Shift in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18167)
*Everest Yang,Skye Thompson,George D. Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究持续基于模型的强化学习中，何时应优先使用近期经验而丢弃旧回放数据。作者提出"变化幅度"与"年龄-陈旧度AUC"两个指标刻画该权衡，并在两种运动形态、两种MBRL算法及Real-World RL基准扰动上加以验证；由于部署机器人上无真实陈旧度标签，还评估了由交互数据构建的估计器能否支撑回放策略选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动力学发生变化时需要从新数据中学习，同时不应轻易丢弃可能仍有用的旧经验。在持续基于模型的强化学习中，动力学变化前收集的回放数据可能拖慢适应，但删除它又无谓地减少了训练数据，若旧动力学再次出现，代价会更高。因此需要理解何时近期经验优于完整历史回放。

Method: 作者提出用两个量刻画上述权衡：一是变化幅度，二是年龄-陈旧度AUC（衡量转换年龄对陈旧与新鲜数据的区分能力）。据此分析丢弃陈旧数据在不同情形下的利弊，并在两种运动形态、两种基于模型的强化学习算法以及Real-World RL基准扰动上进行测试。由于真实机器人上没有真实陈旧度标签，进一步评估由交互数据构建的估计器能否提供选择回放策略所需的量。

Result: 实验表明，遗忘陈旧数据在发生较大且永久的变化后有助于适应，但当动力学重复出现、旧数据重新变得有用时则会带来损害。回放数据的保留取决于变化幅度以及动力学如何演化。基于交互数据的估计器在永久变化后能够提供选择回放策略所需的量。

Conclusion: 回放策略的选择本质上取决于对旧数据何时会帮助或损害学习的预测，而这一判断由变化幅度和动力学演化方式共同决定。

Abstract: Adapting to changes in robot dynamics requires learning from new data without discarding experience that may still be useful. In continual model-based reinforcement learning (RL), replay collected before a dynamics change can slow adaptation, while removing it unnecessarily reduces available training data and can be especially costly if earlier dynamics return. We study when recent transitions are preferable to the full replay history. Two quantities characterize this trade-off: change magnitude and age-staleness area under the curve (AUC), measuring how well transition age separates stale from fresh data. Forgetting stale data helps after large permanent shifts but hurts when dynamics recur and older data becomes useful again. Choosing a replay strategy therefore depends on predicting when older data will help or hurt. We test these effects across two locomotion morphologies, two model-based RL algorithms, and Real-World RL benchmark perturbations. Because ground-truth staleness labels are unavailable on deployed robots, we evaluate whether an estimator built from interaction data can still provide the quantities needed to choose a replay strategy after permanent changes. Our results show that replay retention depends on change magnitude and on how the dynamics evolve.

</details>


### [134] [Approximating High Dimensional Self-Motion Manifolds via Deep Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18169)
*Haitao Gao,Yang Song,Liao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文从概率视角重新定义自运动流形（SMM），将其视为给定目标位姿下关节构型条件后验分布的支撑集，从而把SMM恢复转化为采样加聚类问题，方法不依赖流形维度，首次实现7R机械臂4维SMM近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（零空间延拓、学习类方法）都假设SMM是一条曲线，无法推广到更高冗余阶数；而SMM的高效恢复对于可行且全局最优的运动规划至关重要。

Method: 采用概率视角：SMM是给定目标位姿时关节构型条件后验分布的支撑集，恢复SMM即从学习到的分布中采样，再通过聚类分离其不相连的分量。该表述与流形维度无关，冗余阶数增加时无需改动网络结构。

Result: 在1维SMM上，性能与最新的零空间延拓和学习类方法相当；并且是首个能在位置任务中近似7R机械臂高冗余4维SMM的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为任意冗余阶数的SMM近似提供了统一且可扩展的框架，突破了以往仅限曲线型SMM的局限，并验证了在高维SMM上的可行性。

Abstract: Self-motion manifold (SMM) characterizes the geometric structure of the infinite inverse kinematic solutions set of a redundant manipulator at a fixed end-effector pose, and its efficient recovery underpins feasible and global optimal motion planning. Existing methods such as null-space continuation and learning-based methods are formulated around the assumption that an SMM is a curve, and do not extend to higher redundancy orders. We instead adopt a probabilistic view: SMMs are the support of the conditional posterior over configurations given a target pose, so that recovering it reduces to sampling from a learned distribution and separating its disjoint components by clustering. The formulation is independent of the manifold dimension and requires no architectural change as the redundancy order grows. In this work, we demonstrate that our method can approximate 1-D SMMs with performance comparable to the latest null-space continuation and learning-based approach, and that it is the first method capable of approximating highly redundant 4-D SMMs in a 7R manipulator for position tasks. Project website: \href{https://github.com/accuracy-maker/high-dimenstional-self-motion-manifold-approximation}{https://github.com/accuracy-maker/high-dimenstional-self-motion-manifold-approximation}

</details>


### [135] [TacBPM: A Tactile-conditioned Behavior Prior Model for Dexterous Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18174)
*Jie Yin,Wanli Xing,Zeyuan Zhao,Xuezhou Zhu,Zhijie Deng,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 \method，一种触觉条件行为先验模型，用于灵巧手内重定向操作，通过蒸馏多尺度球体专家到潜在控制器，并让下游策略以残差潜在动作复用固定触觉先验，加速训练并实现稳定策略与仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手内操作需要协调高自由度手指关节，通过间歇性、接触丰富的方式交互。除了目标姿态跟踪外，策略还需发现保持物体稳定的手指步态，并适应几何形状、各向异性、姿态、接触和传感变化。直接使用原始关节命令进行探索效率低、难度大。

Method: 提出 \method，即触觉条件行为先验模型。方法将多尺度球体专家蒸馏为潜在控制器，下游策略通过残差潜在动作复用固定的触觉先验，从而减少从原始关节命令重新探索。该先验以触觉-本体感觉历史为条件，使潜在行为反映当前手-物交互状态。

Result: 在任意姿态迁移（各向异性物体）、指定轴旋转以及手臂-手部 Grasp-to-AnyPose 任务（抓取、抬起、运输并到达新工具几何和泛化放置的目标姿态）上评估。实验表明，该方法加速训练并实现稳定策略，而匹配的原始动作 PPO 接近失败；在手内和手臂-手部任务中均成功实现仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: \method 通过触觉条件的行为先验与残差潜在动作复用，有效提升灵巧重定向操作策略的学习效率与稳定性，并具备实际机器人迁移能力。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires policies that coordinate high-DoF hand joints through intermittent, contact-rich interaction. Beyond target-orientation tracking, such policies must discover finger gaits that preserve object stability while adapting to geometry, anisotropy, pose, contact, and sensing changes. We propose \method, a tactile-conditioned behavior prior model for dexterous reorientation. \method distills multi-scale sphere specialists into a latent controller and lets downstream policies reuse the fixed tactile prior through residual latent actions, reducing renewed exploration from raw joint commands. The prior conditions on tactile-proprioceptive history so latent behavior reflects the current hand-object interaction. We evaluate arbitrary-pose transfer across anisotropic objects, commanded-axis rotation, and an arm-hand Grasp-to-AnyPose task in which the robot must grasp, lift, transport, and reach goal poses for novel tool geometries and generalized placements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method accelerates training and enables stable policies where matched raw-action PPO remains near failure, with successful sim-to-real transfer in in-hand and arm-hand tasks.

</details>


### [136] [OmniRisk: Omnidirectional Trajectory-Risk Learning for Agile Quadrotor Dynamic Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18191)
*Yifan He,Yang Liu,Wenhao Zhao,Hai Lin,Deping Zhang,Mingze Ma,Fei Gao,Huan Yu,Zipeng Dai,Ziming Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniRisk：一种全方位规划框架，离线学习轨迹级风险，实现高效机载避障，相对遭遇速度可达15 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在密集高速场景中避障时反应窗口短，稀疏测距观测无法直接揭示障碍物运动，而在线轨迹优化器难以兼顾扩展性与可靠性。

Method: 用固定维度张量融合LiDAR距离全景、动态掩码与笛卡尔表面速度；构建与障碍速度对齐的非对称风险场；沿预测相对轨迹累积风险形成密集监督；采用双分支循环卷积网络在单次前向传播中预测边界终端状态与动态风险，并在全方位锚点格上选择与闭式重构候选基元。

Result: 该方法去除了在线风险累积，风险推断成本与障碍物数量无关；真实飞行中无需微调即可实现相对遭遇速度高达15 m/s的连续规避机动。

Conclusion: OmniRisk通过离线学习轨迹级风险，实现了高效可靠的高速全方位机载避障。

Abstract: Agile quadrotor avoidance of fast-moving obstacles requires anticipating collisions and selecting feasible maneuvers within short reaction windows. Reliable predictive avoidance remains challenging because sparse range observations do not directly reveal obstacle motion, while online trajectory optimizers either scale poorly with obstacle count or remain efficient at the expense of reliability in dense, high-speed encounters. We present OmniRisk, an omnidirectional planning framework that learns trajectory-level risk offline for efficient onboard evasion. A fixed-dimensional tensor combines LiDAR range panoramas, dynamic masks, and Cartesian surface velocities to represent geometry and motion jointly. We formulate an asymmetric risk field aligned with obstacle velocity that emphasizes approaching interactions and attenuates receding ones. Accumulating this risk along predicted relative trajectories provides dense supervision and discourages unnecessary hesitation after obstacles pass. A dual-branch circular convolutional network predicts terminal boundary states and dynamic risks for candidate primitives over an omnidirectional anchor lattice in a single forward pass, followed by selection and closed-form reconstruction of the selected candidate primitive. This formulation removes online risk accumulation along trajectories and makes risk-inference cost independent of obstacle count. OmniRisk enables efficient onboard avoidance, with real-world flights demonstrating consecutive evasive maneuvers at relative encounter speeds up to 15 m/s without fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/VANdexj/OmniRisk.

</details>


### [137] [WAVE-Go: World-Model Navigation with Adaptive Execution for Wheel-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18193)
*Mingyi Li,Ji Li,Zhihao Ouyang,Yage He,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAVE-Go 提出将世界模型的动作预测与可中断的命令执行分离，通过自适应选择可执行动作前缀并取消失效命令，实现图像目标导航，在动态环境下取得更高成功率与更低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型能预测导航动作后果，但预测的动作序列在执行时可能失效，尤其是轮腿机器人在遇到动态障碍或切换运动模式时。因此需要一种能根据新观测动态调整执行的机制。

Method: WAVE-Go 将世界-动作预测与可中断命令执行分离：执行器自适应选择动作前缀，并在新观测使执行失效时取消待执行命令；采用条件风险公式，在估计的累计失败预算下确定前缀选择；姿态与运动模式切换需通过间隙、稳定性和任务证据检查。

Result: 同分布成功率 74.1%，动态分布外成功率 63.3%，分别超过最强基线 4.7 和 7.7 个百分点；每 100 米碰撞数从 4.4 降至 2.9。相比可中断固定四命令执行，成功率提升 4.0 个百分点，重规划频率降低 51.2%，碰撞率降低 6.5%。执行消融显示运行时中断能提升成功率、碰撞率和反应延迟，但会增加重规划。

Conclusion: 自适应、可中断的执行方式能够在导航性能与规划开销之间取得平衡，支持该框架作为兼顾性能与效率的导航方案。

Abstract: World models can anticipate the consequences of navigation actions, but predicted action sequences may become invalid during execution, especially when wheel-legged robots encounter dynamic obstacles or change locomotion modes. We propose WAVE-Go, an image-goal navigation framework that separates world-action prediction from interruptible command execution. Its executor adaptively selects an action prefix and cancels pending commands when updated observations invalidate execution. A conditional-risk formulation specifies prefix selection under an estimated cumulative failure budget, while posture and locomotion-mode transitions require clearance, stability, and task-evidence checks. In the reported navigation evaluation, WAVE-Go achieves 74.1% in-distribution success and 63.3% dynamic out-of-distribution success, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.7 and 7.7 percentage points, respectively, while reducing collisions from 4.4 to 2.9 per 100 m. Compared with interruptible fixed four-command execution, WAVE-Go raises success by 4.0 percentage points while reducing replanning frequency by 51.2% and collision rate by 6.5%. Execution ablations also show that runtime interruption improves success, collision rate, and reaction latency at the cost of additional replanning. These results support adaptive, interruptible execution as a means of balancing navigation performance and planning overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/vigorlee/wave-go.

</details>


### [138] [WholeBodyWAM: Learning Whole-Body World Action Models with Scalable Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18197)
*Bowei Zhang,Qiyao Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Xinhua Wang,Meng Li,Yilei Wang,Leiwang Zhang,Jian Tang,Lu Zhou,Lei Sun,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WholeBodyWAM类人世界-动作模型，利用大规模异构人体/类人运动数据预训练作为可迁移预测先验，再结合视频与动作专家联合微调，实现全身操作并提升真实任务性能与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人全身操作需要协调的全身动力学，但目标机器人上大规模轨迹采集成本高、难以规模化；而来自人类与类人平台的全身运动数据丰富，却无法直接作为特定本体的机器人动作使用。因此本文探究这些可扩展的运动资源能否为类人世界-动作建模提供可迁移的预测先验。

Method: 构建UniMotion-4K运动语料（超4000小时，涵盖人类视频、原生3D运动数据集与异构类人平台），并将其规范化到统一运动空间；预训练一个语言条件Motion Expert，在无目标机器人动作监督下预测未来全身运动；机器人后训练阶段，通过非对称Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）注意力将预训练Motion Expert与Video、Action Expert集成，使预测场景动力学与全身运动共同指导特定本体的动作生成。

Result: 实验表明，WholeBodyWAM随运动预训练规模增大而持续受益，改善了未来运动预测与下游任务性能，并能有效迁移到真实世界类人操作；预训练运动先验在有限目标机器人示范下显著提升数据效率。

Conclusion: 大规模异构人体/类人运动数据可作为一种可迁移的预测先验，有效支撑类人全身世界-动作建模，减少对目标机器人数据的依赖，并为真实类人操作提供可扩展的预训练路径。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation requires coordinated whole-body dynamics, yet large-scale trajectories from a target robot are expensive to collect and difficult to scale. In contrast, whole-body motion from human and humanoid sources is abundantly available, although such data cannot be directly used as embodiment-specific robot actions. This work asks whether these scalable motion resources can instead provide a transferable predictive prior for humanoid world-action modeling. We introduce WholeBodyWAM, a humanoid world-action model that learns whole-body dynamics from large-scale heterogeneous motion before target-robot training. We curate UniMotion-4K, a motion corpus spanning more than 4K hours from human videos, native 3D motion datasets, and heterogeneous humanoid platforms, and canonicalize these diverse sources into a unified motion space. A language-conditioned Motion Expert is then pretrained to predict future whole-body motion without target-robot action supervision. During robot post-training, the pretrained Motion Expert is integrated with Video and Action Experts through asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention, enabling predictive scene dynamics and whole-body motion to jointly inform embodiment-specific action generation. Experiments show that WholeBodyWAM consistently benefits from increased motion-pretraining scale, improves future-motion prediction and downstream task performance, and transfers effectively to real-world humanoid manipulation. Moreover, the pretrained motion prior substantially improves data efficiency under limited target-robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [139] [Reinforcement Learning for Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18207)
*Perry Dong,Kuo-Han Hung,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Real-Time EXPO-FT，通过将慢速的 VLA 动作生成与快速的编辑策略解耦，实现满足实时控制需求的强化学习微调，显著提升动态操作任务中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练 VLA 模型推理延迟高，执行动作时观测已过时，导致分布偏移，严重削弱可靠性与性能。已有的异步策略执行方法多基于模仿学习，无法超越训练分布以追求更高可靠性。作者希望实现能满足真实世界动态操作实时控制要求的 RL 微调。

Method: 基于 EXPO-FT 框架，将缓慢但富有表达力的动作生成与快速反应式动作编辑解耦：大型预训练 VLA 利用强行为先验提出动作块，轻量级编辑策略根据最新观测，通过编辑动作实现快速的响应式决策。该实例化方法称为 Real-Time EXPO-FT。

Result: 在 Kinetix 基准上，Real-Time EXPO-FT 使延迟策略在 10/10 环境中取得优于延迟与非延迟方法的最佳性能。在四个动态真实任务（机器人传球、平衡球、桌上足球踢球、动态物体抓取）中，在线机器人数据限制为 10 分钟，平均策略性能从 42% 提升至 97%，全程无需人工干预。

Conclusion: Real-Time EXPO-FT 展示了快速、样本高效地适应困难真实动态的能力，为实时 VLA 策略的 RL 微调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning on top of large, pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offers promise for highly reliable robot deployment. However, because of their scale, modern VLA models suffer from high inference latency, so the observation used to select an action is often stale by execution time, creating a distribution shift that can substantially degrade reliability and performance. Prior work has explored asynchronous policy execution to reduce the effect of latency, but these methods are mostly built on imitation learning and offer no mechanism for moving beyond the training distribution toward higher reliability. We close this gap by enabling RL fine-tuning that meets the real-time control requirements of dynamic real-world manipulation. Our approach builds on EXPO-FT, a framework for sample-efficient, reliable VLA fine-tuning with reinforcement learning, and decouples slow, expressive action generation from fast, reactive action edits: a large pretrained VLA proposes action chunks using its strong behavior prior, while a lightweight edit policy performs fast, reactive decision-making by editing actions in response to changes in state, conditioned on the latest observation. We instantiate this as Real-Time EXPO-FT, an RL framework for finetuning real-time VLA policies. On the Kinetix benchmark, Real-Time EXPO-FT enables a delayed policy to achieve the best performance among delayed and non-delayed methods in 10 out of 10 environments. On four dynamic real-world tasks, robot object passing, ball balancing, table soccer kicking, and dynamic object picking, with online robot data capped at 10 minutes, Real-Time EXPO-FT improves average policy performance from 42% to 97%, all without human intervention, demonstrating rapid, sample-efficient adaptation to challenging real-world dynamics. Website: https://pd-perry.github.io/real-time-expo-ft

</details>


### [140] [CANTABILE: Learning Expressive Dynamics for Robotic Piano Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18213)
*Woosik Kim,Wonhyeok Choi,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CANTABILE动力学感知框架，使机器人钢琴演奏不再只看音准，还能表达力度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人钢琴演奏已有基准，但进展几乎只用音符准确率（音高与起始时间）衡量，忽视了音乐力度这一表现力关键要素，导致其既无奖励也无评价。

Method: (i) 将策略条件化于即将到来的速度目标，并把按键起始角速度映射回MIDI力度，闭合乐谱到触键的回路；(ii) 耦合力度保真奖励与起始覆盖奖励，防止通过省略难音符提升力度；(iii) 用α缩放、仅手指的残差精调冻结的力度感知基策略，将击键强度适应与正常音符执行解耦。

Result: 在RoboPianist的力度丰富子集EXPRESSIVE-51上，速度F1（在±8 MIDI力度容差内联合衡量音高、起始与强度）从0.06提升至0.34，全部51首歌曲均改善，匹配音符力度误差减半以上，log-mel距离降低8%；力度随机化训练还支持运行时控制演奏强度而无需重训。

Conclusion: CANTABILE为机器人钢琴演奏引入动力学感知的评估与训练范式，显著提升表现力与力度控制。

Abstract: Robotic piano playing has emerged as a standard benchmark for dexterous bimanual manipulation, yet progress on it has been measured almost entirely by note accuracy -- which keys are pressed (pitch) and when (onset) -- leaving the musical dynamics essential for expressive performance neither rewarded nor evaluated. We propose CANTABILE, a dynamics-aware framework for robotic piano performance that (i) closes the score-to-contact loop by conditioning the policy on upcoming velocity goals and mapping each key's angular velocity at onset back to MIDI velocity, (ii) couples a velocity-fidelity reward with an onset-coverage reward, so that dynamics cannot be improved by omitting difficult notes, and (iii) refines a frozen dynamics-aware base policy with an alpha-scaled, finger-only residual that localizes strike-intensity adaptation away from nominal note execution. On EXPRESSIVE-51, a dynamics-rich 51-song subset of RoboPianist, CANTABILE raises Velocity F1 -- jointly measuring pitch, onset, and intensity within a +/-8 MIDI-velocity tolerance -- from 0.06 to 0.34 over the RoboPianist baseline, improves all 51 songs, more than halves matched-note velocity error, and reduces log-mel distance to reference audio by 8%. Intensity-randomized training further enables runtime control of performance intensity without retraining.

</details>


### [141] [UMI-Bridge: Action-Anchored Latent Alignment across Human and Robot Manipulation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18232)
*Haiyi Liu,Jingming Ma,Ke Rui,Yuteng Wei,Yuan Ma,Yushen Zuo,Honglong Tian,Haoran Jia,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Shiyi Chen,Haiyan Mao,Jiaqi Zhang,Chun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UMI-Bridge，用UMI作为中间域按动作等价对齐表示，实现无需机器人演示的数据高效机器人学习与UMI到机器人的任务迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人演示数据有限，而人类操作数据（如自我中心视频和手持UMI演示）容易采集。但这些数据在视角、本体结构和动作监督上存在差异，难以按照操作动作而非视觉外观对齐表示。

Method: 以UMI为中间域，按动作等价而非像素相似性对齐表示。UMI动作监督将潜在表示锚定到末端执行器运动和夹爪行为，同步的头-腕观测和成对的ego-UMI片段支持跨视角、跨域对齐。在无机器人演示的人类操作数据上训练双视角潜在动作模型（LAM），然后冻结其腕部教师和动力学模型来正则化UMI与机器人数据上的VLA后训练。共享腕部接口在训练时提供跨域监督，同时保持策略的标准推理架构。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务上，UMI-Bridge平均成功率达91.7%，而匹配UMI和机器人数据的朴素协同训练为73.3%。在两个数据效率任务上，仅用25%机器人演示加UMI数据即超过全量数据仅机器人基线。另有两个任务仅从UMI演示学习、无需任务特定机器人演示，成功率达85%和90%。

Conclusion: 结果支持动作锚定的潜表示对齐，可用于数据高效的机器人学习和UMI到机器人的任务迁移。

Abstract: Real-robot demonstrations are limited, motivating the use of human manipulation data collected without robots, including egocentric videos and handheld Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) demonstrations. However, differences in viewpoint, embodiment, and available action supervision make it difficult to align representations across these sources according to manipulation motion rather than visual appearance. We introduce UMI-Bridge, which uses UMI as an intermediate domain to align representations according to action equivalence rather than pixel similarity. UMI action supervision anchors the latent representation to end-effector motion and gripper behavior, while synchronized head-wrist observations and paired ego-UMI clips support alignment across views and domains. We train a dual-view latent action model (LAM) on human manipulation data without robot demonstrations, then freeze its wrist teacher and dynamics model to regularize vision-language-action (VLA) post-training on UMI and robot data. The shared wrist interface enables this training-time supervision across both domains while preserving the policy's standard inference architecture. Across three real-robot tasks, UMI-Bridge achieves 91.7% mean success versus 73.3% for Naive Co-training with matched UMI and robot data. On two data-efficiency tasks, it surpasses a full-data Robot-only baseline using 25% of the robot demonstrations together with UMI data. It also achieves 85% and 90% success on two additional tasks learned from UMI demonstrations without task-specific robot demonstrations. These results support action-anchored latent alignment for data-efficient robot learning and UMI-to-robot task transfer.

</details>


### [142] [ForceDelta-VLA: Distilling Force-Conditioned ActionCorrections for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18242)
*Ju Dong,Yu Fu,Jian Chen,Yimeng Liu,Haocheng Zhao,Lei Zhang,Kaixin Bai,Liding Zhang,Diwen Zheng,Alois Christian Knoll,Angela P. Schoellig,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceDelta-VLA是一种修正蒸馏框架，通过构造显式的力修正目标，让轻量策略基于力历史和机器人状态调整参考动作，在接触丰富的操作任务中显著提升成功率并降低接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务中，将任务级运动与依赖接触的调整融合在单一动作预测里存在问题；而演示数据缺乏显式标签，无法将预测分解为可复用的参考动作与修正项。

Method: 利用冻结教师的力条件模式与学习到的无力条件模式的成对预测，构造显式的力修正目标；另设延迟修正目标以处理参考动作不匹配及参考状态变化；训练采用异步调度回放，并使用执行期缓存的任務上下文；最终得到轻量策略，利用近期力历史和机器人状态调整参考动作，无需重新生成完整动作块。

Result: 在九个单臂和双臂接触丰富任务上，ForceDelta-VLA达到82.2%平均成功率，相比原ForceVLA基线54.4%大幅提升；直接执行第一阶段时间教师为70.6%；相比ForceVLA，完整系统在双臂平台上将成功试验的平均峰值接触力降低约26%。

Conclusion: ForceDelta-VLA通过修正蒸馏实现了参考动作与力修正的显式分离，在提升接触丰富操作成功率的同时有效降低接触力，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Force-aware Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies improve contact-rich manipulation, but typically combine task-level motion and contact-dependent adjustment in a single action prediction. Demonstrations provide no explicit labels for decomposing that prediction into a reusable reference action and a correction. We present ForceDelta-VLA, a correction-distillation framework that constructs an explicit force-correction target using paired predictions from a frozen teacher's force-conditioned and learned force-agnostic modes. A separate delay-correction target accounts for reference-action mismatch and the change in reference state. Training uses asynchronous schedule replay with the cached task context available during execution. The resulting lightweight policy adjusts the reference actions using recent force history and robot state, responding to contact changes between reference-action updates without regenerating complete action chunks. Across nine single-arm and bimanual contact-rich tasks, ForceDelta-VLA achieves an 82.2% mean success rate, compared with 54.4% for the original ForceVLA baseline.
